## Instruction Priority

Superpowers skills override default system prompt behavior, but **user instructions always take precedence**:

1. **User's explicit instructions** (CLAUDE.md, GEMINI.md, AGENTS.md, direct requests) — highest priority
2. **Superpowers skills** — override default system behavior where they conflict
3. **Default system prompt** — lowest priority

If CLAUDE.md, GEMINI.md, or AGENTS.md says "don't use TDD" and a skill says "always use TDD," follow the user's instructions. The user is in control.

## How to Access Skills

**In Claude Code:** Use the `Skill` tool. When you invoke a skill, its content is loaded and presented to you—follow it directly. Never use the Read tool on skill files.

**In Copilot CLI:** Use the `skill` tool. Skills are auto-discovered from installed plugins. The `skill` tool works the same as Claude Code's `Skill` tool.

**In Gemini CLI:** Skills activate via the `activate_skill` tool. Gemini loads skill metadata at session start and activates the full content on demand.

**In other environments:** Check your platform's documentation for how skills are loaded.

## Platform Adaptation

Skills use Claude Code tool names. Non-CC platforms: see `references/copilot-tools.md` (Copilot CLI), `references/codex-tools.md` (Codex) for tool equivalents. Gemini CLI users get the tool mapping loaded automatically via GEMINI.md.

# Using Skills

## The Rule

**Invoke relevant or requested skills BEFORE any response or action.** Even a 1% chance a skill might apply means that you should invoke the skill to check. If an invoked skill turns out to be wrong for the situation, you don't need to use it.

    "Already brainstormed?" -> "Might any skill apply?" [label="yes"];
    "Invoke brainstorming skill" -> "Might any skill apply?";

    "Has checklist?" -> "Create TodoWrite todo per item" [label="yes"];

    "Create TodoWrite todo per item" -> "Follow skill exactly";
}

## Red Flags

These thoughts mean STOP—you're rationalizing:

| Thought | Reality |
|---------|---------|
| "This is just a simple question" | Questions are tasks. Check for skills. |
| "I need more context first" | Skill check comes BEFORE clarifying questions. |
| "Let me explore the codebase first" | Skills tell you HOW to explore. Check first. |
| "I can check git/files quickly" | Files lack conversation context. Check for skills. |
| "Let me gather information first" | Skills tell you HOW to gather information. |
| "This doesn't need a formal skill" | If a skill exists, use it. |
| "I remember this skill" | Skills evolve. Read current version. |
| "This doesn't count as a task" | Action = task. Check for skills. |
| "The skill is overkill" | Simple things become complex. Use it. |
| "I'll just do this one thing first" | Check BEFORE doing anything. |
| "This feels productive" | Undisciplined action wastes time. Skills prevent this. |
| "I know what that means" | Knowing the concept ≠ using the skill. Invoke it. |

## Skill Priority

When multiple skills could apply, use this order:

1. **Process skills first** (brainstorming, debugging) - these determine HOW to approach the task
2. **Implementation skills second** (frontend-design, mcp-builder) - these guide execution

"Let's build X" → brainstorming first, then implementation skills.
"Fix this bug" → debugging first, then domain-specific skills.

## Skill Types

**Rigid** (TDD, debugging): Follow exactly. Don't adapt away discipline.

**Flexible** (patterns): Adapt principles to context.

The skill itself tells you which.

## User Instructions

Instructions say WHAT, not HOW. "Add X" or "Fix Y" doesn't mean skip workflows.

---
name: verification-before-completion
description: Use when about to claim work is complete, fixed, or passing, before committing or creating PRs - requires running verification commands and confirming output before making any success claims; evidence before assertions always
---

# Verification Before Completion

## Overview

Claiming work is complete without verification is dishonesty, not efficiency.

**Core principle:** Evidence before claims, always.

**Violating the letter of this rule is violating the spirit of this rule.**

## The Iron Law

```
NO COMPLETION CLAIMS WITHOUT FRESH VERIFICATION EVIDENCE
```

If you haven't run the verification command in this message, you cannot claim it passes.

## The Gate Function

```
BEFORE claiming any status or expressing satisfaction:

1. IDENTIFY: What command proves this claim?
2. RUN: Execute the FULL command (fresh, complete)
3. READ: Full output, check exit code, count failures
4. VERIFY: Does output confirm the claim?
   - If NO: State actual status with evidence
   - If YES: State claim WITH evidence
5. ONLY THEN: Make the claim

Skip any step = lying, not verifying
```

## Common Failures

| Claim | Requires | Not Sufficient |
|-------|----------|----------------|
| Tests pass | Test command output: 0 failures | Previous run, "should pass" |
| Linter clean | Linter output: 0 errors | Partial check, extrapolation |
| Build succeeds | Build command: exit 0 | Linter passing, logs look good |
| Bug fixed | Test original symptom: passes | Code changed, assumed fixed |
| Regression test works | Red-green cycle verified | Test passes once |
| Agent completed | VCS diff shows changes | Agent reports "success" |
| Requirements met | Line-by-line checklist | Tests passing |

## Red Flags - STOP

- Using "should", "probably", "seems to"
- Expressing satisfaction before verification ("Great!", "Perfect!", "Done!", etc.)
- About to commit/push/PR without verification
- Trusting agent success reports
- Relying on partial verification
- Thinking "just this once"
- Tired and wanting work over
- **ANY wording implying success without having run verification**

## Rationalization Prevention

| Excuse | Reality |
|--------|---------|
| "Should work now" | RUN the verification |
| "I'm confident" | Confidence ≠ evidence |
| "Just this once" | No exceptions |
| "Linter passed" | Linter ≠ compiler |
| "Agent said success" | Verify independently |
| "I'm tired" | Exhaustion ≠ excuse |
| "Partial check is enough" | Partial proves nothing |
| "Different words so rule doesn't apply" | Spirit over letter |

## Key Patterns

**Tests:**
```
✅ [Run test command] [See: 34/34 pass] "All tests pass"
❌ "Should pass now" / "Looks correct"
```

**Regression tests (TDD Red-Green):**
```
✅ Write → Run (pass) → Revert fix → Run (MUST FAIL) → Restore → Run (pass)
❌ "I've written a regression test" (without red-green verification)
```

**Build:**
```
✅ [Run build] [See: exit 0] "Build passes"
❌ "Linter passed" (linter doesn't check compilation)
```

**Requirements:**
```
✅ Re-read plan → Create checklist → Verify each → Report gaps or completion
❌ "Tests pass, phase complete"
```

**Agent delegation:**
```
✅ Agent reports success → Check VCS diff → Verify changes → Report actual state
❌ Trust agent report
```

## Why This Matters

From 24 failure memories:
- your human partner said "I don't believe you" - trust broken
- Undefined functions shipped - would crash
- Missing requirements shipped - incomplete features
- Time wasted on false completion → redirect → rework
- Violates: "Honesty is a core value. If you lie, you'll be replaced."

## When To Apply

**ALWAYS before:**
- ANY variation of success/completion claims
- ANY expression of satisfaction
- ANY positive statement about work state
- Committing, PR creation, task completion
- Moving to next task
- Delegating to agents

**Rule applies to:**
- Exact phrases
- Paraphrases and synonyms
- Implications of success
- ANY communication suggesting completion/correctness

## The Bottom Line

**No shortcuts for verification.**

Run the command. Read the output. THEN claim the result.

This is non-negotiable.

# CLAUDE.md

> **GENERATED FROM agent-contract.md — DO NOT EDIT BY HAND.**
>
> Edit `agent-contract.md` then run `pnpm generate:configs`. CI fails if drift detected.

This file is the Claude Code entry point for the **SelfKnowledgeBaseWeb** monorepo. It tells
every Claude session what the project is, the hard rules, the agent roster, and the workflow.

- Spec: [docs/superpowers/specs/2026-04-29-self-knowledge-base-design.md](docs/superpowers/specs/2026-04-29-self-knowledge-base-design.md)
- Active plan: [docs/plans/active.md](docs/plans/active.md)
- Single source for agents: [agent-contract.md](agent-contract.md)
- Codex tool invocations: [docs/runbooks/codex-tool-invocations.md](docs/runbooks/codex-tool-invocations.md)

## Hard rules

1. **File size**: 200 lines (target) / 300 lines (ESLint `max-lines` warn) / 500 lines
   (`pnpm size-check` hard fail). Spec §3.6.
2. **Cross-file references**: every doc/code change must keep doc cross-references and
   contract files in sync (`packages/*/CONTRACT.md`, `agent-contract.md`).
3. **Lychee link-check**: broken markdown links block merge. Run `pnpm link-check` locally.
4. **`pnpm check` is mandatory before requesting review** (lint + typecheck + test + build + size).
5. **No direct git from workers**: only `git-operator` may commit / branch / rebase / push.
6. **No web access from workers**: only `researcher` may `web_search` / `web_fetch`.
7. **No cross-package moves from workers**: only `refactorer` may reorganize packages,
   and every reorganization requires an ADR under `docs/decisions/`.

## Commands cheat-sheet

| Command                 | Purpose                                               |
| ----------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------- |
| `pnpm install`          | Install workspace dependencies                        |
| `pnpm dev`              | Run all `dev` scripts (parallel)                      |
| `pnpm check`            | Lint + typecheck + test + build + size-check          |
| `pnpm check:affected`   | Same, scoped to packages affected since `origin/main` |
| `pnpm size-check`       | Hard 500-line limit on every source file              |
| `pnpm link-check`       | Lychee scan over `./**/*.md`                          |
| `pnpm generate:configs` | Re-derive everything from `agent-contract.md`         |
| `pnpm format`           | Prettier write across the repo                        |

## Review workflow (spec §3.2 + ADR-0007 D2)

```
worker (writes code)
   │
   ▼
[Bash] codex exec --profile code-reviewer  (Codex 5.3-spark, line-level rigor, cheap default)
   │
   ├── if high-risk (D2 rows 1/2/4/8) → [Bash] codex exec --profile pr-gate  (Codex 5.5, deep scan)
   │
   ▼
pr-reviewer    (Claude, selective per ADR-0007 D2; spec match + regression + arch consistency)
   │
   ▼
git-operator   (Claude, only authorized git surface, runs `pnpm check` once more)
```

High-risk triggers per ADR-0007 D2 (4 rows +pr-gate +pr-reviewer, 4 rows +pr-reviewer only):
- **+pr-gate +pr-reviewer** (D2 rows 1/2/4/8): contract change, package add/remove, new ADR required, CI/deploy/auth/security touch.
- **+pr-reviewer only, skip pr-gate** (D2 rows 3/5/6/7): spec/agent-contract/ADR edit, delete/rename package, cross ≥3 packages, performance-auditor flagged.

See [ADR-0007 D2](docs/decisions/ADR-0007-job-function-codex-heavy-execution.md).

Codex tools (code-reviewer / pr-gate / plan-challenger / 5 scaffolders) are
[orchestrator-direct Bash invocations](docs/runbooks/codex-tool-invocations.md) post ADR-0007 D5,
not teammate spawns.

     1	# @skb/block-pdf Contract
     2	
     3	PDF render block (Wave 2 Track D2). 第二个 `render-block-eng` 包；hand-craft
     4	（与 D1 block-math 同 owner，独立实现，不走 codex-block-generator）。
     5	浏览器原生 PDF viewer 是 runtime authority — 由 iframe + PDF Open Parameters
     6	fragment 驱动；本包不在 npm 拉 react-pdf / pdfjs-dist 渲染依赖（ADR-0008 D1
     7	dead-dep policy + Astro SSR 不可执行 react-pdf 的 worker 双重约束）。
     8	
     9	## Public surface
    10	
    11	五个 entry：
    12	
    13	- `.` (root barrel) — re-export `./core`
    14	- `./core` — headless 层
    15	  - `pdfCore: BlockCoreDefinition<typeof propsSchema>` — name=`'pdf'` / kind=`'render'` / mdxComponent=`'Pdf'`
    16	  - `serializePdf(node) → mdxJsxFlowElement` — Tiptap → mdast (Wave 3 mdx-bridge routing 时启用)
    17	  - `parsePdf(mdast) → TiptapNode` — mdast → Tiptap (同上；处理 `searchable` boolean shorthand + `page` 数字 coerce)
    18	  - 类型: `PdfTiptapNode` / `PdfMdastJsxElement`
    19	- `./ui-default` — presentational 层
    20	  - `pdfUiDefault: BlockUIDefinition<typeof pdfCore.propsSchema>` — uiId=`'default'`
    21	  - `PdfEditorView` / `PdfRenderView` — `ComponentType<BlockViewProps<...>>`
    22	  - `renderPdf(src, page, searchable) → PdfRenderDescriptor` — single-source 渲染描述器（**single authority** for Pdf.tsx + Pdf.astro，定义在 `src/ui-default/render-pdf.ts`）
    23	  - `PDF_TOKENS: PdfTokens` — design-token name witnesses（`@skb/design-tokens` 类型绑定）
    24	- `./ui-default/Pdf.astro` — apps/site SSR consumer 直接 import 的 Astro 组件（亦走 `renderPdf` authority）
    25	- `./ui-default/pdf.css` — 视觉规则单一来源（design-token-bound，border / bg / error 走 `var(--color-*)`）
    26	
    27	### Consumer 使用方式
    28	
    29	`apps/site` / `editor-shell` 必须 `import '@skb/block-pdf/ui-default/pdf.css';`
    30	方能渲染 iframe 容器边框 / 背景 / 间距 token。Astro page 直接
    31	`import Pdf from '@skb/block-pdf/ui-default/Pdf.astro';`。consumer 不需要
    32	import 任何 PDF 渲染库 —— 浏览器原生 viewer 处理 iframe 内容。
    33	
    34	`propsSchema` 形状（见 `src/core/core-definition.ts`）：
    35	
    36	```typescript
    37	z.object({
    38	  src: z.string().min(1),
    39	  page: z.number().int().min(1).default(1),
    40	  searchable: z.boolean().default(false),
    41	}).strict()
    42	```
    43	
    44	## Invariants
    45	
    46	继承自 [block-foundation/CONTRACT.md](../block-foundation/CONTRACT.md#invariants)：
    47	`Defensive copy` / `Schema strictness` / `Default UI lookup` / `Serialize / parse hook ownership`
    48	（即 `BlockRegistry.listCores()` / `listUIs(coreName)` defensive-copy 行为、
    49	`propsSchema` 必须 `.strict()`、同 `coreName` 多 UI 时 `getUI(coreName)` 取首注册 `uiId`、
    50	serialize/parse 由本包 `core/` own 不在 foundation 注册）。
    51	
    52	block-pdf 特定不变量：
    53	
    54	- **`coreName='pdf'` (kebab-case)** + **`mdxComponent='Pdf'` (PascalCase)**:
    55	  `pdfCore.name`/`.mdxComponent` 字面值固定，与 BlockRegistry / mdx-bridge 路由
    56	  ([RFC §1](../block-foundation/RFC.md#1-core-side)) 一致
    57	- **Hook 命名 verb-as-prefix**: `serializePdf` / `parsePdf` / `renderPdf`
    58	  ([RFC §5](../block-foundation/RFC.md#5-serialize--parse-hook-ownership-scope-clarification))
    59	- **`uiId='default'` reserved**: inherit foundation 的 `Default UI lookup` 不变量（首注册即 default）
    60	- **浏览器原生 PDF viewer 是 runtime authority** + **renderPdf single authority**:
    61	  `src/ui-default/render-pdf.ts` 唯一构造 `${src}#page=${page}` PDF Open Parameters
    62	  fragment + data-attr map（ADR-0006 item #5 algorithm replication —
    63	  authority = browser embedded PDF viewer）。Pdf.tsx + Pdf.astro 都 import
    64	  `./render-pdf`；ssr-render.test.ts 5-row corpus 检测 inline 漂移
    65	- **No DIY PDF parser / canvas rasterizer**: 本包不实现 PDF 渲染逻辑，不
    66	  bundle pdfjs-dist 用于 viewer。`searchable=true` 走 build-time
    67	  `scripts/extract-pdf-text.ts`（pdfjs-dist legacy build 用于文本抽取，runtime 不
    68	  shared），与渲染路径完全解耦
    69	- **Headless 自给**: `core/` 不 import React / Tiptap / PDF 库；仅依赖
    70	  `@skb/block-foundation` + `zod`（ADR-0003 D1）。`ui-default/` 才 import React +
    71	  design-tokens
    72	- **propsSchema single authority**: 仅在 `src/core/core-definition.ts` 定义；
    73	  ui-default / serialize / parse / 测试均 import `pdfCore.propsSchema`（ADR-0006 item #4）
    74	- **Self-validating serialize/parse**: `serializePdf` / `parsePdf` 各自调
    75	  `propsSchema.parse`（parse 侧 `searchable` 先 null/string coerce → boolean，
    76	  `page` 先 string coerce → number）；契约偏离在 mdx-bridge 路由前 throw
    77	- **错误显式（不静默）**: 缺失 / 不可读 PDF 由浏览器原生 viewer 显示空白文档
    78	  UI；本包不 silent-fallback 到 alt 文本 —— 容器 + iframe shell 始终渲染，
    79	  让用户看见加载失败状态（spec D2 等价 D1 "red error box with the source visible"
    80	  原则的 PDF 等价物）
    81	
    82	## Wave 3 mdx-bridge 路由集成（pending）
    83	
    84	当前 `serializePdf` / `parsePdf` 是 stub —— 暴露稳定签名，未被 mdx-bridge 消费。
    85	Wave 3 mdx-bridge routing table PR 会：
    86	
    87	1. 在 `mdx-bridge/parse.ts` 的 `mdastBlockToTiptap` 拦截 `mdxJsxFlowElement{name:'Pdf'}` →
    88	   `parsePdf(node)` → 把返回 `PdfTiptapNode` 嵌入 doc.content
    89	2. 在 `mdx-bridge/serialize.ts` 的 `tiptapToMdastBlock` 拦截 `type='pdf'` →
    90	   `serializePdf(node)` → 返回 `mdxJsxFlowElement` 进 mdast
    91	3. pdf block 无 children，递归不展开
    92	
    93	## Wave 3 search-index 集成（pending, `searchable=true`）
    94	
    95	`searchable: true` 是 build-time hint，runtime no-op（Wave 2）。Wave 3
    96	search-index PR 将：
    97	
    98	1. apps/site build pipeline 扫描所有 `<Pdf searchable .../>` 实例
    99	2. 调 [`scripts/extract-pdf-text.ts`](../../scripts/extract-pdf-text.ts) 的
   100	   `extractPdfText({ pdfPath: src })` API 抽取 PDF 文本
   101	3. 把抽出文本写入 search index（文档 id ↔ src ↔ 全文 mapping）
   102	4. 提供 search UI 查询入口
   103	
   104	Wave 2 仅暴露 `searchable` schema 字段 + parse/serialize round-trip
   105	+ runtime 无副作用；mdx-doctor 不会因为 `searchable` 在 props 而 fail
   106	（schema 已声明 default=false）。
   107

   111	- **apps/site SSR**: import `@skb/block-pdf/ui-default/Pdf.astro`；consumer 同 PR 加 pdf.css import
   112	- **Wave 3 mdx-bridge integration**: 见上节
   113	- **Wave 3 search-index integration**: 见上节

     1	# @skb/block-foundation Contract
     2	
     3	## Public surface
     4	
     5	- `BlockRegistry` class — 双层注册表（ADR-0003）
     6	  - `registerCore(core)` / `registerUI(ui)`
     7	  - `getCore(name)` / `listCores()`
     8	  - `getUI(coreName, uiId?)` / `listUIs(coreName)`
     9	- `defineCore(def)` / `defineUI(def)` factory helpers
    10	- `BlockCoreDefinition<T>` / `BlockUIDefinition<T>` / `BlockViewProps<T>` interfaces
    11	- `BlockKind = 'prose' | 'component' | 'render' | 'viz'` （四分类，Wave 2 由 2→4 additive 扩展，ratified by [ADR-0009](../../docs/decisions/ADR-0009-block-kind-union-expansion.md)）
    12	  - `'prose'` — Wave 1 default for headless prose blocks (无 MDX 组件，由 `proseExtensions` 提供 markdown 行为)
    13	  - `'component'` — Wave 1 for MDX-component blocks（block-callout / block-code / block-image — Track C simple-block-eng）
    14	  - `'render'` — **Wave 2 NEW** for hand-crafted render blocks consuming external runtime authority（block-math KaTeX / block-pdf react-pdf — Track D render-block-eng）
    15	  - `'viz'` — **Wave 2 NEW** for visualization blocks consuming heavy runtime libs（block-jupyter Pyodide / block-nn-viz TensorFlow.js / block-agent-flow React Flow — Track E viz-block-eng / kernel consumer）
    16	- `proseExtensions` — Tiptap 扩展数组，提供全部 markdown 行为
    17	
    18	## Invariants
    19	
    20	- `BlockKind` 四分不可破坏：新 block 必须明确归属 `prose` / `component` / `render` / `viz` 之一。Wave 1 仅 `prose` + `component` 二分；Wave 2 additive 扩展加 `render` + `viz`（由 `render-block-eng` 与 `viz-block-eng` 工种 own，与 agent-contract.md Tier 1 worker 分工一致；扩展授权见 [ADR-0009](../../docs/decisions/ADR-0009-block-kind-union-expansion.md)）。kind 定义不可被进一步窄化（不允许把 `render` 重新归并入 `component`），扩展再加 kind 需 ADR
    21	- Prose blocks 零自写代码——任何看似需要新 prose block 的场景应通过组合 `proseExtensions` 内现有扩展或追加单条 Tiptap 扩展实现，不再加 prose-kind block
    22	- `BlockCoreDefinition.mdxComponent` 必须 PascalCase，且与 MDX 文件 import 中使用的名字一致
    23	- Core 与 UI 物理分离（ADR-0003）：core 不允许 import 任何 React/Tiptap 视觉 API；UI 必须 import core（不允许 inline 重复 schema）
    24	- 同 core 多 UI 时 `getUI(name)` 取首个注册：约定首个 uiId 为 `'default'`；adopter 在 register 顺序上需谨慎
    25	- **Defensive copy**：`listCores()` 与 `listUIs(coreName)` 每次返回 fresh array。Caller 修改返回值不影响 registry 内部 state。Implementation: `src/registry.ts:84` (`listCores`) + `src/registry.ts:96` (`listUIs`)；regression: `src/__tests__/registry.test.ts` "listCores returns a defensive copy" + "listUIs returns a defensive copy"
    26	- **Idempotent register**：`registerCore` 同 `name` 第二次调用 throws `Duplicate core name: <name>`；`registerUI` 同 `(coreName, uiId)` 第二次 throws `Duplicate UI registration: core=<coreName> uiId=<uiId>`。避免 silent override；实现见 `src/registry.ts:60-63` + `src/registry.ts:71-75`
    27	- **Schema strictness**：每个 `BlockCoreDefinition.propsSchema` 必须是 `z.object(...).strict()`；嵌套 `ZodObject` 同样必须 `.strict()`（[ADR-0006](../../docs/decisions/ADR-0006-asymmetry-audit-checklist.md) item #3 trip-hazard：`.strict()` 不传播）。当前类型仅约束到 `ZodTypeAny`（type-narrow 到 `ZodObject` 待 Wave 3）；Wave 2 行为约定 + reviewer + RFC.md §1 显式提醒。未 `.strict()` 不会 throw，但 `propsSchema.parse(input)` 会 silently strip unknown keys，引发 contract drift
    28	- **Serialize / parse hook ownership**：MDX serialize/parse 由各 `block-*` 包的 `core/` own（ADR-0003 D1+D2），不在 `block-foundation` 注册。`block-foundation` 仅 own `BlockCoreDefinition` 形状（含 `mdxComponent` 字符串）；`mdx-bridge` 通过 `mdxJsxFlowElement.name` 字符串路由到对应 block 的 export（命名约定 verb-as-prefix：`serialize<BlockName>` / `parse<BlockName>`，与 mdx-bridge 现有 `mdxToTiptap` / `tiptapToMdx` 同 style）。详见 RFC.md §"5. Serialize / parse hook ownership"
    29	
    30	## Forward-compat consumers (Wave 2+)
    31	
    32	Wave 2 中段当 `BlockRegistry` 开始用 `@skb/content-types` 的 schema 验证
    33	注册的 block props（如跨 block 共享的 frontmatter / metadata 形状），届时
    34	`@skb/content-types` 将被同 PR 加入 `package.json#dependencies` +
    35	`tsconfig.json#references`，并出现源码 `import { ... } from '@skb/content-types'`。
    36	
    37	当前 Wave 1 close 状态：源码无 content-types import，按
    38	[ADR-0008](../../docs/decisions/ADR-0008-wave-2-entry-policies.md) D1
    39	（dead-dep policy = tighten），dep 与 ref 均未声明；forward-compat 意图
    40	仅在本 prose 段表达，不在 package.json / tsconfig 占位。
    41	
    42	## How to register a new `block-*` package
    43	
    44	step-by-step walkthrough (core / ui-default / 测试样板 / 错误场景 / serialize-parse 归属)
    45	for the first real consumer 在 [RFC.md](./RFC.md) — 第一个 `block-*` PR
    46	(Task C1 / C2) 必须 reference 此 RFC；后续 `block-*` PR 沿用相同模式。
    47	RFC 是 consumer-facing 教程，CONTRACT.md 是 public API 形状 + 关键不变量
    48	的权威；二者补充而非重叠（[ADR-0008 D2](../../docs/decisions/ADR-0008-wave-2-entry-policies.md)）。
    49	
    50	## Modifying this file
    51	
    52	公共表面（`BlockCoreDefinition` / `BlockUIDefinition` / `BlockRegistry` / `BlockKind` / `BlockViewProps` / `defineCore` / `defineUI` / `proseExtensions`）的任何字段或方法增删改一律需 ADR — **包括添加 optional 字段、改 `proseExtensions` 数组（增删 Tiptap 扩展、顺序变更、`as const` shape 变化）**。spec §2.3 把 block-foundation 列为接口包，规则"改动需 ADR；全员同步"不区分 optional vs required。
    53	
    54	原因：optional 字段会传播到 mdx-bridge serialization + 8 个 Wave 2 block 的 core+ui 实现 + apps/site frontmatter schema；`proseExtensions` 改动直接影响 mdx-bridge round-trip fixtures + editor-shell parsing 行为；无 ADR 协调会引发漂移。
    55	
    56	## Related

     1	# @skb/block-math Contract
     2	
     3	KaTeX-backed math block (Wave 2 Track D1）。第一个 `render-block-eng` 包；
     4	hand-craft（不走 codex-block-generator），block-pdf (D2) 同 owner 独立 hand-craft。
     5	
     6	## Public surface
     7	
     8	五个 entry：
     9	
    10	- `.` (root barrel) — re-export `./core`
    11	- `./core` — headless 层

    13	  - `serializeMath(node) → mdxJsxFlowElement` — Tiptap → mdast (Wave 3 mdx-bridge routing 时启用)
    14	  - `parseMath(mdast) → TiptapNode` — mdast → Tiptap (同上)
    15	  - 类型: `MathTiptapNode` / `MathMdastJsxElement`
    16	- `./ui-default` — presentational 层

    18	  - `MathEditorView` / `MathRenderView` — `ComponentType<BlockViewProps<...>>`
    19	  - `renderMath(expression, display) → string` — KaTeX HTML 字符串生成器（**single authority** for editor + SSR + NodeView，定义在 `src/ui-default/render-math.ts`）
    20	  - `MATH_TOKENS: MathTokens` — design-token name witnesses（`@skb/design-tokens` 类型绑定）
    21	- `./ui-default/Math.astro` — apps/site SSR consumer 直接 import 的 Astro 组件（亦走 `renderMath` authority）

    23	
    24	### Consumer 使用方式
    25	
    26	`apps/site` / `editor-shell` 必须 `import '@skb/block-math/ui-default/math.css';`
    27	方能渲染 KaTeX 错误态样式以及 display/inline 间距 token。同时
    28	consumers 必须 `import 'katex/dist/katex.css';`（KaTeX 自身字体 + 内部布局；
    29	本包不重发包，避免重复加载）。Astro page 直接 `import Math from '@skb/block-math/ui-default/Math.astro';`。
    30	
    31	`propsSchema` 形状（见 `src/core/core-definition.ts`）：
    32	
    33	```typescript
    34	z.object({
    35	  expression: z.string().min(1),
    36	  display: z.boolean().default(false),
    37	}).strict()
    38	```

    44	（即 `BlockRegistry.listCores()` / `listUIs(coreName)` defensive-copy 行为、
    45	`propsSchema` 必须 `.strict()`、同 `coreName` 多 UI 时 `getUI(coreName)` 取首注册 `uiId`、
    46	serialize/parse 由本包 `core/` own 不在 foundation 注册）。

    50	- **`coreName='math'` (kebab-case)** + **`mdxComponent='Math'` (PascalCase)**:
    51	  `mathCore.name`/`.mdxComponent` 字面值固定，与 BlockRegistry / mdx-bridge 路由 ([RFC §1](../block-foundation/RFC.md#1-core-side)) 一致
    52	- **Hook 命名 verb-as-prefix**: `serializeMath` / `parseMath` / `renderMath` ([RFC §5](../block-foundation/RFC.md#5-serialize--parse-hook-ownership-scope-clarification))
    53	- **`uiId='default'` reserved**: inherit foundation 的 `Default UI lookup` 不变量（首注册即 default）
    54	- **KaTeX 是 runtime authority** + **renderMath single authority**: `src/ui-default/render-math.ts`
    55	  唯一 wrap `katex.renderToString`（ADR-0006 item #5 algorithm replication —
    56	  authority = `katex` npm 包）。Math.tsx + Math.astro 都 import `./render-math`；
    57	  ssr-render.test.ts 4-row corpus 检测 inline 漂移
    58	- **Headless 自给**: `core/` 不 import React / Tiptap / KaTeX；仅依赖 `@skb/block-foundation` + `zod`（ADR-0003 D1）。`ui-default/` 才 import React + katex + design-tokens
    59	- **propsSchema single authority**: 仅在 `src/core/core-definition.ts` 定义；ui-default / serialize / parse / 测试均 import `mathCore.propsSchema`（ADR-0006 item #4）
    60	- **Self-validating serialize/parse**: `serializeMath` / `parseMath` 各自调 `propsSchema.parse`（parse 侧 display 先字符串 coerce → boolean）；契约偏离在 mdx-bridge 路由前 throw
    61	- **错误显式（不静默）**: KaTeX 配 `throwOnError: false` + `strict: 'ignore'`：
    62	  软错误内联 `color:#cc0000` 片段（source visible），硬错误 `.katex-error` class；
    63	  `math.css` 两条路径都 escalate 到 `--color-error` token 的 bordered box（spec D1 要求），不许 silent fallback
    64	
    65	## Wave 3 mdx-bridge 路由集成（pending）
    66	
    67	当前 `serializeMath` / `parseMath` 是 stub —— 暴露稳定签名，未被 mdx-bridge 消费。
    68	Wave 3 mdx-bridge routing table PR 会：
    69	
    70	1. 在 `mdx-bridge/parse.ts` 的 `mdastBlockToTiptap` 拦截 `mdxJsxFlowElement{name:'Math'}` →
    71	   `parseMath(node)` → 把返回 `MathTiptapNode` 嵌入 doc.content
    72	2. 在 `mdx-bridge/serialize.ts` 的 `tiptapToMdastBlock` 拦截 `type='math'` →
    73	   `serializeMath(node)` → 返回 `mdxJsxFlowElement` 进 mdast
    74	3. math block 无 children，递归不展开
    75	
    76	## Forward-compat consumers

    79	- **apps/site SSR**: import `@skb/block-math/ui-default/Math.astro`；consumer 同 PR 加 katex CSS import

/bin/bash -lc 'nl -ba packages/block-pdf/src/ui-default/render-pdf.ts' in /home/weiyi/selfKnowledgeBaseWeb
 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	/**
     2	 * Single authority for PDF rendering across this package (ADR-0006 item #5).
     3	 * Pdf.tsx (React/NodeView path) and Pdf.astro (SSR path) MUST both import
     4	 * `renderPdf` from here — never inline iframe `src` construction or container
     5	 * markup with their own logic. ssr-render.test.ts exercises a corpus to detect
     6	 * drift if a future edit accidentally re-inlines the URL builder.
     7	 *
     8	 * Why iframe (not react-pdf) for both paths:
     9	 * - Browser-native PDF viewer is the runtime authority (no in-package canvas
    10	 *   rasterizer). All major browsers handle `application/pdf` via embedded
    11	 *   viewer; the `#page=N` fragment is part of the
    12	 *   [PDF Open Parameters](https://opensource.adobe.com/dc-acrobat-sdk-docs/library/pdfsdk/pdfopenparams.html)
    13	 *   convention recognized by Chrome/Edge/Firefox/Safari.
    14	 * - Astro SSR cannot run react-pdf (it requires a browser worker thread +
    15	 *   canvas APIs). Iframe yields byte-equivalent SSR + client output, satisfying
    16	 *   the D1 single-source invariant pattern.
    17	 * - Failure mode is graceful: missing/inaccessible `src` shows the browser's
    18	 *   own empty-doc UI; the wrapper container retains a stable shell + bordered
    19	 *   viewport via design-token CSS for the empty-state error path.
    20	 *
    21	 * Output shape contract:
    22	 * - `src`: original `propsSchema.src` (URL or path) verbatim
    23	 * - `page`: validated 1-based int (propsSchema floor = 1)
    24	 * - `searchable`: build-time hint (Wave 3 search-index integration); runtime
    25	 *   no-op in Wave 2
    26	 * - `iframeSrc`: `${src}#page=${page}` — PDF Open Parameters fragment
    27	 * - `dataAttrs`: stable `data-*` attribute map for Tag wrapper, used by
    28	 *   pdf.css selectors. Keys are kebab-case strings ready for set:html /
    29	 *   spread.
    30	 */
    31	export interface PdfRenderDescriptor {
    32	  readonly src: string;
    33	  readonly page: number;
    34	  readonly searchable: boolean;
    35	  readonly iframeSrc: string;
    36	  readonly dataAttrs: {
    37	    readonly 'data-block': 'pdf';
    38	    readonly 'data-page': string;
    39	    readonly 'data-searchable': string;
    40	  };
    41	}
    42	
    43	export const renderPdf = (
    44	  src: string,
    45	  page: number,
    46	  searchable: boolean,
    47	): PdfRenderDescriptor => ({
    48	  src,
    49	  page,
    50	  searchable,
    51	  iframeSrc: `${src}#page=${page}`,
    52	  dataAttrs: {
    53	    'data-block': 'pdf',
    54	    'data-page': String(page),
    55	    'data-searchable': String(searchable),
    56	  },
    57	});

 succeeded in 110ms:
     1	import { pdfCore } from './core-definition';
     2	import type { PdfMdastJsxElement, PdfTiptapNode } from './serialize';
     3	
     4	/**
     5	 * MDX 解析 stub。Wave 3 mdx-bridge 在 mdastBlockToTiptap 看到
     6	 * mdxJsxFlowElement{name:'Pdf'} 时按 mdxComponent 路由到本函数（RFC §5）。
     7	 *
     8	 * `searchable` 三种 MDX 形式 (mdast-util-mdx-jsx convention):
     9	 *   - `<Pdf searchable>` (boolean shorthand)        → attr.value === null  → true
    10	 *   - `<Pdf searchable="true">`                     → attr.value === "true" → true
    11	 *   - `<Pdf searchable="false">`                    → attr.value === "false"→ false
    12	 * 任何其他形式（数字 / 非 true/false 字符串）throw — 不静默 false。
    13	 *
    14	 * `page` 字符串走 Number coerce → propsSchema.parse 校验 int.min(1)。非整数 / NaN
    15	 * 由 zod 抛错，保持 explicit-error invariant。
    16	 *
    17	 * 其他 attr (如 src) 一律期望 string；null shorthand 仅 searchable 适用。
    18	 */
    19	export function parsePdf(node: PdfMdastJsxElement): PdfTiptapNode {
    20	  if (node.name !== pdfCore.mdxComponent) {
    21	    throw new Error(
    22	      `parsePdf: expected mdxComponent="${pdfCore.mdxComponent}", got "${node.name}"`,
    23	    );
    24	  }
    25	  const rawProps: Record<string, string | number | boolean> = {};
    26	  for (const attr of node.attributes) {
    27	    if (attr.name === 'searchable') {
    28	      if (attr.value === null) {
    29	        rawProps[attr.name] = true;
    30	      } else if (attr.value === 'true' || attr.value === 'false') {
    31	        rawProps[attr.name] = attr.value === 'true';
    32	      } else {
    33	        throw new Error(
    34	          `parsePdf: invalid searchable attribute value: ${JSON.stringify(attr.value)}`,
    35	        );
    36	      }
    37	    } else if (attr.name === 'page') {
    38	      if (attr.value === null) {
    39	        throw new Error(
    40	          `parsePdf: attribute "page" must have a string value (got null shorthand; only "searchable" supports boolean shorthand)`,
    41	        );
    42	      }
    43	      const numeric = Number(attr.value);
    44	      if (!Number.isFinite(numeric)) {
    45	        throw new Error(
    46	          `parsePdf: page attribute must be a finite number, got ${JSON.stringify(attr.value)}`,
    47	        );
    48	      }
    49	      rawProps[attr.name] = numeric;
    50	    } else {
    51	      if (attr.value === null) {
    52	        throw new Error(
    53	          `parsePdf: attribute "${attr.name}" must have a string value (got null shorthand; only "searchable" supports boolean shorthand)`,
    54	        );
    55	      }
    56	      rawProps[attr.name] = attr.value;
    57	    }
    58	  }
    59	  const validated = pdfCore.propsSchema.parse(rawProps);
    60	  return {
    61	    type: 'pdf',
    62	    attrs: {
    63	      src: validated.src,
    64	      page: validated.page,
    65	      searchable: validated.searchable,
    66	    },
    67	  };
    68	}

 succeeded in 104ms:
     1	import { pdfCore } from './core-definition';
     2	
     3	/**
     4	 * MDX 序列化 stub。完整实现等 Wave 3 mdx-bridge routing table 落地（RFC §5）：
     5	 * mdx-bridge 拿到 TiptapNode{type:'pdf'} 时按 mdxComponent 字符串路由到本函数。
     6	 *
     7	 * Wave 2 阶段仅暴露稳定签名；mdx-bridge 还未引用此 export，所以 stub 抛错足够。
     8	 */
     9	export interface PdfTiptapNode {
    10	  readonly type: 'pdf';
    11	  readonly attrs: {
    12	    readonly src: string;
    13	    readonly page: number;
    14	    readonly searchable: boolean;
    15	  };
    16	}
    17	
    18	/**
    19	 * `value` is `string | null` per mdast-util-mdx-jsx convention:
    20	 *   - `<Pdf searchable>` (boolean shorthand) → attribute with `value: null`
    21	 *   - `<Pdf searchable="true">` / `<Pdf searchable="false">` → string
    22	 *   - `<Pdf src="...">` → string (always)
    23	 *   - `<Pdf page="2">` → string (numeric coerced on parse)
    24	 * `parsePdf` handles boolean shorthand for `searchable`; `serializePdf` always
    25	 * emits string form (`String(boolean)` / `String(number)`) for round-trip
    26	 * stability.
    27	 */
    28	export interface PdfMdastJsxElement {
    29	  readonly type: 'mdxJsxFlowElement';
    30	  readonly name: string;
    31	  readonly attributes: ReadonlyArray<{
    32	    readonly type: 'mdxJsxAttribute';
    33	    readonly name: string;
    34	    readonly value: string | null;
    35	  }>;
    36	  readonly children: readonly unknown[];
    37	}
    38	
    39	export function serializePdf(node: PdfTiptapNode): PdfMdastJsxElement {
    40	  const validated = pdfCore.propsSchema.parse(node.attrs);
    41	  return {
    42	    type: 'mdxJsxFlowElement',
    43	    name: pdfCore.mdxComponent,
    44	    attributes: [
    45	      { type: 'mdxJsxAttribute', name: 'src', value: validated.src },
    46	      { type: 'mdxJsxAttribute', name: 'page', value: String(validated.page) },
    47	      {
    48	        type: 'mdxJsxAttribute',
    49	        name: 'searchable',
    50	        value: String(validated.searchable),
    51	      },
    52	    ],
    53	    children: [],
    54	  };
    55	}

     1	import type { BlockViewProps } from '@skb/block-foundation';
     2	import { pdfCore } from '../core/core-definition';
     3	import { renderPdf } from './render-pdf';
     4	
     5	/**
     6	 * PdfView 接受 inferred props ({ src, page, searchable }) — 与
     7	 * `pdfCore.propsSchema` 的 ZodInfer 等价。函数式组件而非
     8	 * `ComponentType<BlockViewProps<typeof ...>>` 显式标注，避免 generic 变 invariant
     9	 * 在 BlockRegistry.registerUI 处与 ZodTypeAny widening 冲突（exactOptionalPropertyTypes 触发）。
    10	 *
    11	 * iframe 渲染走 `./render-pdf.ts` 单一权威，与 Pdf.astro SSR path 同源
    12	 * （ADR-0006 item #5 algorithm replication）。浏览器原生 PDF viewer 是 runtime
    13	 * authority；空 src 由 Pdf.astro 的 fallback box 处理（在 Astro layer，因
    14	 * propsSchema.src.min(1) 已先 throw 在 build 前）。
    15	 *
    16	 * `searchable: true` 是 Wave 3 build-time search-index 提示
    17	 * （consumer = scripts/extract-pdf-text.ts），runtime no-op。
    18	 */
    19	export function PdfView({
    20	  props,
    21	}: BlockViewProps<typeof pdfCore.propsSchema>): JSX.Element {
    22	  const desc = renderPdf(props.src, props.page, props.searchable);
    23	  return (
    24	    <div
    25	      data-block={desc.dataAttrs['data-block']}
    26	      data-page={desc.dataAttrs['data-page']}
    27	      data-searchable={desc.dataAttrs['data-searchable']}
    28	    >
    29	      <iframe
    30	        src={desc.iframeSrc}
    31	        title={`PDF: ${desc.src}`}
    32	        loading="lazy"
    33	      />
    34	    </div>
    35	  );
    36	}
    37	
    38	export const PdfEditorView = PdfView;
    39	export const PdfRenderView = PdfView;

    33	 * 错误态: 浏览器原生 PDF viewer 自身已对不可读 / 跨域 / 404 PDF 显示空白。
    34	 * 本规则提供包裹层 fallback 视觉提示（aria-hidden 占位伪元素，禁用 :empty
    35	 * 误判 — Chromium iframe 即使加载失败仍非 :empty）。Wave 3 search-index
    36	 * 集成时（searchable=true）会有更主动的 fallback；Wave 2 仅保留 box
    37	 * 一致性。
    38	 */
    39	[data-block='pdf'][data-searchable='true']::after {
    40	  content: '';
    41	  display: none;
    42	}

     4	/**
     5	 * Byte-equivalence corpus for renderPdf authority (ADR-0006 item #5).
     6	 *
     7	 * `renderPdf` is the single import point for iframe URL + data-attr
     8	 * construction in this package; both Pdf.tsx (React/NodeView path) and
     9	 * Pdf.astro (SSR path) call it. This suite exercises a representative input
    10	 * corpus to:
    11	 *   1. catch silent regressions if a future edit re-inlines the URL builder
    12	 *      (drift detector for the algorithm-replication audit);
    13	 *   2. assert PDF Open Parameters fragment shape (`#page=N`) — required for
    14	 *      browser-native PDF viewer to honor initial page;
    15	 *   3. cover the missing-file fallback path: even with a bogus `src`, the
    16	 *      descriptor is structurally valid so the wrapper container + iframe
    17	 *      shell render and the browser's native viewer surfaces its own
    18	 *      empty-state UI.
    19	 *
    20	 * Browser-native PDF viewer is the runtime authority — these tests do NOT
    21	 * pin the exact rendered PDF visuals (browsers diverge); they assert
    22	 * structural invariants (iframeSrc fragment, data-attr shape, identity of
    23	 * src round-trip).
    24	 */
    25	describe('renderPdf (iframe SSR authority)', () => {
    26	  it('produces iframeSrc with #page=N fragment for default page=1', () => {
    27	    const desc = renderPdf('/files/paper.pdf', 1, false);
    28	    expect(desc.iframeSrc).toBe('/files/paper.pdf#page=1');
    29	    expect(desc.dataAttrs['data-block']).toBe('pdf');
    30	    expect(desc.dataAttrs['data-page']).toBe('1');
    31	    expect(desc.dataAttrs['data-searchable']).toBe('false');
    32	  });
    33	
    34	  it('produces iframeSrc with #page=N for arbitrary page', () => {
    35	    const desc = renderPdf('https://example.com/doc.pdf', 42, true);
    36	    expect(desc.iframeSrc).toBe('https://example.com/doc.pdf#page=42');
    37	    expect(desc.dataAttrs['data-page']).toBe('42');
    38	    expect(desc.dataAttrs['data-searchable']).toBe('true');
    39	  });
    40	
    41	  it('preserves src verbatim (no encoding / normalization in render layer)', () => {
    42	    // src normalization is propsSchema's job; renderPdf is downstream and must
    43	    // not double-normalize. Test pinning string identity.
    44	    const desc = renderPdf('/files/My Doc.pdf', 1, false);
    45	    expect(desc.src).toBe('/files/My Doc.pdf');
    46	    expect(desc.iframeSrc).toBe('/files/My Doc.pdf#page=1');
    47	  });
    48	
    49	  it('handles missing-file-style src (browser viewer surfaces its own empty state)', () => {
    50	    // Even with bogus src, the descriptor is structurally valid — Pdf.tsx /
    51	    // Pdf.astro both render an iframe shell + container; the browser's native
    52	    // PDF viewer renders its own empty-doc UI on the inside. Wave 2 invariant:
    53	    // never silent-fallback to alt text; always show the iframe shell so users
    54	    // see the failure.
    55	    const desc = renderPdf('/does-not-exist.pdf', 1, false);

     1	import katex from 'katex';
     2	
     3	/**
     4	 * Single authority for KaTeX rendering across this package (ADR-0006 item #5).
     5	 * Math.tsx (React/NodeView path) and Math.astro (SSR path) MUST both import
     6	 * `renderMath` from here — never inline `katex.renderToString` with their own
     7	 * options literal. The byte-equivalence between editor / SSR / NodeView output
     8	 * follows from this single import. ssr-render.test.ts exercises a corpus to
     9	 * detect drift if a future edit accidentally re-inlines the call.
    10	 *
    11	 * Options policy:
    12	 * - `displayMode`: caller-controlled (inline vs block).
    13	 * - `throwOnError: false`: KaTeX renders invalid LaTeX as `.katex-error` red box;
    14	 *   error-explicit (math.css binds `.katex-error` to `--color-error` token).
    15	 * - `output: 'html'`: HTML-only (no MathML); SSR + React DOM both consume HTML
    16	 *   string. Matches Math.astro's `set:html` path.
    17	 * - `strict: 'ignore'`: KaTeX warnings (deprecated commands, unicode chars) do
    18	 *   not throw; consistent with `throwOnError: false`.
    19	 */
    20	export const renderMath = (expression: string, display: boolean): string =>
    21	  katex.renderToString(expression, {
    22	    displayMode: display,
    23	    throwOnError: false,
    24	    output: 'html',
    25	    strict: 'ignore',
    26	  });

 succeeded in 136ms:
     1	import { mathCore } from './core-definition';
     2	import type { MathMdastJsxElement, MathTiptapNode } from './serialize';
     3	
     4	/**
     5	 * MDX 解析 stub。Wave 3 mdx-bridge 在 mdastBlockToTiptap 看到
     6	 * mdxJsxFlowElement{name:'Math'} 时按 mdxComponent 路由到本函数（RFC §5）。
     7	 *
     8	 * `display` 三种 MDX 形式 (mdast-util-mdx-jsx convention):
     9	 *   - `<Math display>` (boolean shorthand)        → attr.value === null  → true
    10	 *   - `<Math display="true">`                     → attr.value === "true" → true
    11	 *   - `<Math display="false">`                    → attr.value === "false"→ false
    12	 * 任何其他形式（数字 / 非 true/false 字符串）throw — 不静默 false。
    13	 *
    14	 * 其他 attr (如 expression) 一律期望 string；null shorthand 仅 display 适用。
    15	 */
    16	export function parseMath(node: MathMdastJsxElement): MathTiptapNode {
    17	  if (node.name !== mathCore.mdxComponent) {
    18	    throw new Error(
    19	      `parseMath: expected mdxComponent="${mathCore.mdxComponent}", got "${node.name}"`,
    20	    );
    21	  }
    22	  const rawProps: Record<string, string | boolean> = {};
    23	  for (const attr of node.attributes) {
    24	    if (attr.name === 'display') {
    25	      if (attr.value === null) {
    26	        rawProps[attr.name] = true;
    27	      } else if (attr.value === 'true' || attr.value === 'false') {
    28	        rawProps[attr.name] = attr.value === 'true';
    29	      } else {
    30	        throw new Error(
    31	          `parseMath: invalid display attribute value: ${JSON.stringify(attr.value)}`,
    32	        );
    33	      }
    34	    } else {
    35	      if (attr.value === null) {
    36	        throw new Error(
    37	          `parseMath: attribute "${attr.name}" must have a string value (got null shorthand; only "display" supports boolean shorthand)`,
    38	        );
    39	      }
    40	      rawProps[attr.name] = attr.value;
    41	    }
    42	  }
    43	  const validated = mathCore.propsSchema.parse(rawProps);
    44	  return {
    45	    type: 'math',
    46	    attrs: { expression: validated.expression, display: validated.display },
    47	  };
    48	}

 succeeded in 123ms:
     1	import type { BlockViewProps } from '@skb/block-foundation';
     2	import { mathCore } from '../core/core-definition';
     3	import { renderMath } from './render-math';
     4	
     5	/**
     6	 * MathView 接受 inferred props ({ expression, display }) — 与
     7	 * `mathCore.propsSchema` 的 ZodInfer 等价。函数式组件而非
     8	 * `ComponentType<BlockViewProps<typeof ...>>` 显式标注，避免 generic 变 invariant
     9	 * 在 BlockRegistry.registerUI 处与 ZodTypeAny widening 冲突（exactOptionalPropertyTypes 触发）。
    10	 *
    11	 * KaTeX 渲染走 `./render-math.ts` 单一权威，与 Math.astro SSR path 同源
    12	 * （ADR-0006 item #5 algorithm replication）。错误态由 KaTeX 渲染为
    13	 * `.katex-error` 红色块（math.css 绑 `--color-error` token）。
    14	 */
    15	export function MathView({
    16	  props,
    17	}: BlockViewProps<typeof mathCore.propsSchema>): JSX.Element {
    18	  const html = renderMath(props.expression, props.display);
    19	  const Tag = props.display ? 'div' : 'span';
    20	  return (
    21	    <Tag
    22	      data-block="math"
    23	      data-display={props.display ? 'true' : 'false'}
    24	      // KaTeX 输出受信（npm 包内部生成），无 user HTML 注入路径
    25	      dangerouslySetInnerHTML={{ __html: html }}
    26	    />
    27	  );
    28	}
    29	
    30	export const MathEditorView = MathView;
    31	export const MathRenderView = MathView;

    12	// （ADR-0006 item #5 algorithm replication：authority = renderMath；不允许在

    17	---
    18	
    19	<Tag data-block="math" data-display={String(display)} set:html={html} />

 succeeded in 119ms:
     1	import type { ZodTypeAny, infer as ZodInfer } from 'zod';
     2	import type { ComponentType } from 'react';
     3	
     4	export type BlockKind = 'prose' | 'component' | 'render' | 'viz';
     5	
     6	/**
     7	 * BlockCore：headless 层 —— 无 React 依赖，纯逻辑（props schema + MDX 序列化在
     8	 * mdx-bridge 处理）。Core 可被 Adopter 复用并配自定义 UI。
     9	 */
    10	export interface BlockCoreDefinition<TSchema extends ZodTypeAny = ZodTypeAny> {
    11	  /** kebab-case identifier，全局唯一 */
    12	  readonly name: string;
    13	  readonly kind: BlockKind;
    14	  readonly propsSchema: TSchema;
    15	  /** MDX 中实际使用的 PascalCase 组件名，例如 "Callout" / "JupyterBlock" */
    16	  readonly mdxComponent: string;
    17	}
    18	
    19	export interface BlockViewProps<TSchema extends ZodTypeAny> {
    20	  readonly props: ZodInfer<TSchema>;
    21	  readonly content?: string;
    22	  /** Wave 2 起追加 runtime context（kernel session / theme / etc.） */
    23	}
    24	
    25	/**
    26	 * BlockUI：presentational 层 —— React 组件，绑定到已注册的 core。
    27	 * 同一 core 可挂多个 UI（uiId 区分），Adopter 可写自己的 UI 替代 ui-default。
    28	 */
    29	export interface BlockUIDefinition<TSchema extends ZodTypeAny = ZodTypeAny> {
    30	  /** 必须指向已 registerCore 的 core */
    31	  readonly coreName: string;
    32	  /** UI 实现 id：'default' / 'minimal' / 用户自定义 */
    33	  readonly uiId: string;
    34	  readonly EditorView: ComponentType<BlockViewProps<TSchema>>;
    35	  readonly RenderView: ComponentType<BlockViewProps<TSchema>>;
    36	}
    37	
    38	export function defineCore<T extends ZodTypeAny>(
    39	  def: BlockCoreDefinition<T>,
    40	): BlockCoreDefinition<T> {
    41	  return def;
    42	}
    43	
    44	export function defineUI<T extends ZodTypeAny>(
    45	  def: BlockUIDefinition<T>,
    46	): BlockUIDefinition<T> {
    47	  return def;
    48	}
    49	
    50	/**
    51	 * BlockRegistry：双层注册表（ADR-0003）。
    52	 * registerCore：挂业务定义；registerUI：挂视觉实现。
    53	 * 同 core 多 UI 场景：getUI(name) 不带 uiId 时取首个注册的（约定为 'default'）。
    54	 */
    55	export class BlockRegistry {
    56	  readonly #cores = new Map<string, BlockCoreDefinition>();
    57	  readonly #uis = new Map<string, BlockUIDefinition[]>();
    58	
    59	  registerCore(core: BlockCoreDefinition): void {
    60	    if (this.#cores.has(core.name)) {
    61	      throw new Error(`Duplicate core name: ${core.name}`);
    62	    }
    63	    this.#cores.set(core.name, core);
    64	  }
    65	
    66	  registerUI(ui: BlockUIDefinition): void {
    67	    if (!this.#cores.has(ui.coreName)) {
    68	      throw new Error(`Unknown core for UI registration: ${ui.coreName}`);
    69	    }
    70	    const existing = this.#uis.get(ui.coreName) ?? [];
    71	    if (existing.some((u) => u.uiId === ui.uiId)) {
    72	      throw new Error(
    73	        `Duplicate UI registration: core=${ui.coreName} uiId=${ui.uiId}`,
    74	      );
    75	    }
    76	    existing.push(ui);
    77	    this.#uis.set(ui.coreName, existing);
    78	  }
    79	
    80	  getCore(name: string): BlockCoreDefinition | undefined {
    81	    return this.#cores.get(name);
    82	  }
    83	
    84	  listCores(): readonly BlockCoreDefinition[] {
    85	    return [...this.#cores.values()];
    86	  }
    87	
    88	  /** 不传 uiId 时返回首个（约定为 'default'）；传了精确匹配 */
    89	  getUI(coreName: string, uiId?: string): BlockUIDefinition | undefined {
    90	    const list = this.#uis.get(coreName);
    91	    if (!list || list.length === 0) return undefined;
    92	    if (uiId === undefined) return list[0];
    93	    return list.find((u) => u.uiId === uiId);
    94	  }
    95	
    96	  listUIs(coreName: string): readonly BlockUIDefinition[] {
    97	    return [...(this.#uis.get(coreName) ?? [])];
    98	  }
    99	}

 succeeded in 113ms:
     1	import StarterKit from '@tiptap/starter-kit';
     2	import TaskList from '@tiptap/extension-task-list';
     3	import TaskItem from '@tiptap/extension-task-item';
     4	import Typography from '@tiptap/extension-typography';
     5	import { Markdown } from 'tiptap-markdown';
     6	
     7	/**
     8	 * Tiptap extensions providing all prose-level markdown behavior:
     9	 * paragraph / heading / list / quote / code / link / 强调样式 / typography /
    10	 * task-list / markdown input rules / markdown serialization.
    11	 *
    12	 * Spec §1.5: prose blocks are zero-self-written; this list is the contract.
    13	 */
    14	export const proseExtensions = [
    15	  StarterKit,
    16	  TaskList,
    17	  TaskItem.configure({ nested: true }),
    18	  Typography,

     1	# RFC: How a `block-*` package registers with `@skb/block-foundation`
     2	
     3	> **Audience**: `simple-block-eng` / `render-block-eng` / `viz-block-eng` writing
     4	> `block-callout` / `block-code` / `block-image` / `block-math` / `block-pdf` /
     5	> `block-jupyter` / `block-nn-viz` / `block-agent-flow` cores; `ux-ui-lead`
     6	> writing the matching `ui-default/` halves.
     7	>
     8	> **Authority**: [ADR-0008](../../docs/decisions/ADR-0008-wave-2-entry-policies.md)
     9	> D2 freezes the registration interface as of 2026-04-30 (commit `b3581bc`).
    10	> This RFC is the consumer-facing walkthrough; the public API surface itself
    11	> lives in [`CONTRACT.md`](./CONTRACT.md).
    12	
    13	This RFC is **not** a spec — `CONTRACT.md` is. It is a step-by-step recipe
    14	showing how the first real consumer (`block-callout`, Task C1/C2) wires its
    15	core + UI into a `BlockRegistry`. Subsequent `block-*` PRs follow the same
    16	shape; cloned `block-code` / `block-image` cores (Tasks C3-C5b) inherit
    17	this structure verbatim.
    18	
    19	## 1. core/ side
    20	
    21	`core/` is the headless half (ADR-0003). No React, no Tiptap; just a Zod
    22	schema + identifying metadata. Place it at `packages/<block>/src/core/`
    23	and export through the package's `core` subpath.
    24	
    25	```typescript
    26	// packages/block-callout/src/core/core-definition.ts
    27	import { defineCore, type BlockCoreDefinition } from '@skb/block-foundation';
    28	import { z } from 'zod';
    29	
    30	const propsSchema = z
    31	  .object({
    32	    variant: z.enum(['note', 'tip', 'warning', 'danger']),
    33	    title: z.string().optional(),
    34	  })
    35	  .strict();
    36	
    37	export const calloutCore: BlockCoreDefinition<typeof propsSchema> = defineCore({
    38	  name: 'callout',
    39	  kind: 'component',
    40	  propsSchema,
    41	  mdxComponent: 'Callout',
    42	});
    43	```
    44	
    45	Notes that map directly to `BlockCoreDefinition` fields in
    46	[`src/registry.ts`](./src/registry.ts):
    47	
    48	- `name`: kebab-case, globally unique. Used as the registry key — duplicate
    49	  registration throws `Duplicate core name: <name>` at runtime.
    50	- `kind`: `'prose' | 'component' | 'render' | 'viz'` (4-way Wave 2 union; Wave 1
    51	  was binary `'prose' | 'component'` — additive expansion in Wave 2 authorized by
    52	  [ADR-0009](../../docs/decisions/ADR-0009-block-kind-union-expansion.md)). Track C
    53	  simple-block-eng owns `'component'` (block-callout / block-code / block-image —
    54	  composable JSX with rich children); Track D render-block-eng owns `'render'`
    55	  (block-math / block-pdf — visual rendering of a pure declarative input via
    56	  external runtime authority like KaTeX or react-pdf); Track E viz-block-eng /
    57	  kernel consumer owns `'viz'` (block-jupyter / block-nn-viz / block-agent-flow —
    58	  interactive visualization with heavy runtime libs). `'prose'` is reserved for
    59	  behaviors covered by `proseExtensions` and is closed to new entries (CONTRACT
    60	  invariant: "Prose blocks 零自写代码").
    61	- `propsSchema`: must be a `z.object(...).strict()`; every nested
    62	  `ZodObject` must independently call `.strict()`. See
    63	  [`CONTRACT.md` § Invariants — Schema strictness](./CONTRACT.md#invariants)
    64	  + [ADR-0006](../../docs/decisions/ADR-0006-asymmetry-audit-checklist.md) item #3
    65	  (`.strict()` does not propagate into nested objects). The current type
    66	  signature `BlockCoreDefinition<TSchema extends ZodTypeAny>` is wider than
    67	  `ZodObject`; type-narrowing is deferred to Wave 3, so for Wave 2 this is
    68	  a behavior contract enforced by RFC + reviewer + integration tests, not
    69	  by TypeScript. Skipping `.strict()` does not throw — `propsSchema.parse`
    70	  silently strips unknown keys, which is exactly the contract-drift class
    71	  this rule prevents.
    72	- `mdxComponent`: PascalCase, must match the JSX tag used in `.mdx` files
    73	  exactly (the MDX bridge uses this string when serializing back). For
    74	  `block-callout`, MDX content reads `<Callout variant="note">…</Callout>`
    75	  so this field is `'Callout'`.
    76	
    77	`defineCore` is a no-op identity helper that exists purely to give TypeScript
    78	inference a hand — the returned object satisfies `BlockCoreDefinition<T>`
    79	with `T` inferred from `propsSchema`. Equivalent to writing the type
    80	annotation manually. `defineUI` (used in §2) is the symmetric helper for
    81	`BlockUIDefinition<T>`; both helpers exist for inference convenience only,
    82	so consumers can write `defineUI({ ... })` without manually spelling
    83	`<typeof calloutCore.propsSchema>`.
    84	
    85	## 2. ui-default/ side
    86	
    87	`ui-default/` is the presentational half. It imports the core (never inline-
    88	redefines its schema — see ADR-0006 item #4: "single-authority schema → audit
    89	every consumer for inline duplicates"), then attaches React components for
    90	the editor and renderer.
    91	
    92	```typescript
    93	// packages/block-callout/src/ui-default/index.ts
    94	import { defineUI, type BlockUIDefinition } from '@skb/block-foundation';
    95	import { calloutCore } from '../core/core-definition';
    96	import { CalloutEditorView } from './editor-view';
    97	import { CalloutRenderView } from './render-view';
    98	
    99	export const calloutUIDefault: BlockUIDefinition<
   100	  typeof calloutCore.propsSchema
   101	> = defineUI({
   102	  coreName: calloutCore.name, // must reference an already-registered core
   103	  uiId: 'default', // convention: first-registered UI is 'default'
   104	  EditorView: CalloutEditorView,
   105	  RenderView: CalloutRenderView,
   106	});
   107	```
   108	
   109	The two view components share the `BlockViewProps<TSchema>` shape from
   110	[`src/registry.ts`](./src/registry.ts):
   111	
   112	```typescript
   113	// packages/block-callout/src/ui-default/editor-view.tsx
   114	import type { ComponentType } from 'react';
   115	import type { BlockViewProps } from '@skb/block-foundation';
   116	import { calloutCore } from '../core/core-definition';
   117	
   118	export const CalloutEditorView: ComponentType<
   119	  BlockViewProps<typeof calloutCore.propsSchema>
   120	> = ({ props, content }) => {
   121	  // props.variant is z-inferred to 'note' | 'tip' | 'warning' | 'danger'
   122	  // props.title is z-inferred to string | undefined
   123	  // content is the optional Tiptap inner content as a string
   124	  return (
   125	    <aside data-variant={props.variant}>
   126	      {props.title ? <strong>{props.title}</strong> : null}
   127	      <div>{content}</div>
   128	    </aside>
   129	  );
   130	};
   131	```
   132	
   133	`RenderView` follows the same shape but renders for static output (Astro
   134	build / SSR path) rather than the editor. UX/visual conventions for the
   135	component itself (tokens, spacing, accessibility) are owned by `ux-ui-lead`
   136	via `packages/design-tokens/CONTRACT.md`; this RFC fixes only the
   137	registration plumbing.
   138	
   139	### Wiring at boot
   140	
   141	`registerCore` must precede `registerUI` for the same `coreName`; otherwise
   142	`registerUI` throws `Unknown core for UI registration: <coreName>`. The
   143	boot path (Wave 3 `editor-shell` / `apps/site` integration) does:
   144	
   145	```typescript
   146	import { BlockRegistry } from '@skb/block-foundation';
   147	import { calloutCore } from '@skb/block-callout/core';
   148	import { calloutUIDefault } from '@skb/block-callout/ui-default';
   149	
   150	const registry = new BlockRegistry();
   151	registry.registerCore(calloutCore);
   152	registry.registerUI(calloutUIDefault);
   153	
   154	// later, render-time lookup:
   155	const ui = registry.getUI('callout'); // returns calloutUIDefault (uiId='default')
   156	const ui2 = registry.getUI('callout', 'minimal'); // exact match by uiId
   157	```
   158	
   159	When multiple UIs are registered for one core, `getUI(name)` without a
   160	`uiId` returns the **first registered** (CONTRACT invariant — convention:
   161	register `'default'` first). `getUI(name, uiId)` does exact match.
   162	
   163	## 3. Consumer-side test template
   164	
   165	Each `block-*` package ships an integration test that wires its own core +
   166	UI into a fresh registry and verifies the round-trip. This is the minimum
   167	surface; package-specific tests (props validation, schema edge cases, view
   168	rendering) live alongside.
   169	
   170	```typescript
   171	// packages/block-callout/src/__tests__/registry-integration.test.ts
   172	import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
   173	import { BlockRegistry } from '@skb/block-foundation';
   174	import { calloutCore } from '../core/core-definition';
   175	import { calloutUIDefault } from '../ui-default';
   176	
   177	describe('block-callout registry integration', () => {
   178	  it('registers core + UI and resolves both', () => {
   179	    const reg = new BlockRegistry();
   180	    reg.registerCore(calloutCore);
   181	    reg.registerUI(calloutUIDefault);
   182	
   183	    expect(reg.getCore('callout')).toBe(calloutCore);
   184	    expect(reg.getUI('callout')).toBe(calloutUIDefault);
   185	    expect(reg.getUI('callout', 'default')).toBe(calloutUIDefault);
   186	  });
   187	
   188	  it('propsSchema accepts every declared variant', () => {
   189	    for (const variant of ['note', 'tip', 'warning', 'danger'] as const) {
   190	      const parsed = calloutCore.propsSchema.parse({ variant });
   191	      expect(parsed.variant).toBe(variant);
   192	    }
   193	  });
   194	
   195	  it('propsSchema rejects unknown variants and unknown keys', () => {
   196	    expect(() => calloutCore.propsSchema.parse({ variant: 'bogus' })).toThrow();
   197	    expect(() =>
   198	      calloutCore.propsSchema.parse({ variant: 'note', extra: 1 }),
   199	    ).toThrow(); // .strict() — extra key rejected
   200	  });
   201	});
   202	```
   203	
   204	The patterns mirror
   205	[`src/__tests__/registry.test.ts`](./src/__tests__/registry.test.ts) in this
   206	package — the foundation owns registry-mechanics tests; each `block-*` owns
   207	the integration test for its own core+UI pair.
   208	
   209	## 4. Error scenarios
   210	
   211	### 4.1 Runtime errors thrown by `BlockRegistry`
   212	
   213	These come from the methods in [`src/registry.ts`](./src/registry.ts). Workers
   214	should write tests asserting each one fires for their block where applicable.
   215	
   216	| Scenario                                        | Method        | Error message                                              |
   217	| ----------------------------------------------- | ------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------- |
   218	| Register two cores with the same `name`         | `registerCore` | `Duplicate core name: <name>`                              |
   219	| `registerUI` before `registerCore` (same name)  | `registerUI`   | `Unknown core for UI registration: <coreName>`             |
   220	| Two UIs with same `(coreName, uiId)`            | `registerUI`   | `Duplicate UI registration: core=<coreName> uiId=<uiId>`   |
   221	| `getCore(name)` for an unregistered name        | `getCore`      | returns `undefined` (no throw)                             |
   222	| `getUI(coreName)` with no UIs registered        | `getUI`        | returns `undefined` (no throw)                             |
   223	| `getUI(coreName, uiId)` with non-matching uiId  | `getUI`        | returns `undefined` (no throw)                             |
   224	
   225	### 4.2 Compile-time errors (caught by `pnpm typecheck`)
   226	
   227	`BlockCoreDefinition` field errors:
   228	
   229	| Scenario                              | TypeScript error (representative)                                  |
   230	| ------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------ |
   231	| `name` missing                        | `Property 'name' is missing in type ...`                           |
   232	| `name` not a `string`                 | `Type '<X>' is not assignable to type 'string'`                    |
   233	| `kind` missing                        | `Property 'kind' is missing in type ...`                           |
   234	| `kind` not `'prose' \| 'component' \| 'render' \| 'viz'` | `Type '"<X>"' is not assignable to type 'BlockKind'`    |
   235	| `propsSchema` missing                 | `Property 'propsSchema' is missing in type ...`                    |
   236	| `propsSchema` not a Zod schema        | `Type '<X>' is not assignable to type 'ZodTypeAny'`                |
   237	| `mdxComponent` missing                | `Property 'mdxComponent' is missing in type ...`                   |
   238	| `mdxComponent` not a `string`         | `Type '<X>' is not assignable to type 'string'`                    |
   239	
   240	`BlockUIDefinition` field errors (symmetric to `BlockCoreDefinition`: 4 fields × {missing, wrong-type} = 8 rows + props-shape divergence):
   241	
   242	| Scenario                                                | TypeScript error (representative)                                                            |
   243	| ------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
   244	| `coreName` missing                                      | `Property 'coreName' is missing in type ...`                                                 |
   245	| `coreName` not a `string`                               | `Type '<X>' is not assignable to type 'string'`                                              |
   246	| `uiId` missing                                          | `Property 'uiId' is missing in type ...`                                                     |
   247	| `uiId` not a `string`                                   | `Type '<X>' is not assignable to type 'string'`                                              |
   248	| `EditorView` missing                                    | `Property 'EditorView' is missing in type ...`                                               |
   249	| `EditorView` not a `ComponentType<BlockViewProps<T>>`   | `Type '<X>' is not assignable to type 'ComponentType<BlockViewProps<TSchema>>'`              |
   250	| `RenderView` missing                                    | `Property 'RenderView' is missing in type ...`                                               |
   251	| `RenderView` not a `ComponentType<BlockViewProps<T>>`   | `Type '<X>' is not assignable to type 'ComponentType<BlockViewProps<TSchema>>'`              |
   252	| `props` shape passed to view diverges from `TSchema`    | `Type '<inferred>' does not satisfy ...`（出现在 view 实现内 destructure / use point）       |
   253	
   254	What TypeScript does NOT catch (covered by runtime throws above + integration test + reviewer):
   255	
   256	- `coreName` typo (it's a `string`; runtime `Unknown core for UI registration: ...` catches at boot)
   257	- `name` collision across packages (runtime `Duplicate core name: ...` catches at register time)
   258	- `mdxComponent` PascalCase casing (string-level; reviewer + integration test catches)
   259	- `propsSchema` is a Zod schema but **not** `z.object(...).strict()` (e.g. `z.string()`, `z.array(...)`, or `z.object(...)` without `.strict()`): the type signature only requires `ZodTypeAny`, so TypeScript accepts it; `propsSchema.parse(input)` does not throw but silently strips unknown keys, which is the contract-drift risk [`CONTRACT.md` § Invariants — Schema strictness](./CONTRACT.md#invariants) closes via reviewer + integration test (type-narrow to `ZodObject` deferred to Wave 3)
   260	
   261	### 4.3 Defensive-copy guarantee
   262	
   263	`listCores()` and `listUIs(coreName)` return fresh arrays per call (CONTRACT
   264	[Invariants](./CONTRACT.md#invariants) — "Defensive copy"). Consumers can
   265	freely mutate the returned arrays without leaking into the registry. Don't
   266	write tests that assume identity (`reg.listCores() === reg.listCores()`) —
   267	they're new arrays each time. Implementation: `src/registry.ts:84` + `:96`.
   268	
   269	## 5. Serialize / parse hook ownership (scope clarification)
   270	
   271	`block-foundation` does **not** own MDX serialize/parse. Each `block-*`
   272	package owns its own `core/serialize.ts` + `core/parse.ts`; `mdx-bridge`
   273	routes between them via the `mdxComponent` string when it encounters an
   274	`mdxJsxFlowElement` in a parsed MDX document. This split mirrors
   275	[ADR-0003 D1+D2](../../docs/decisions/ADR-0003-headless-presentational-split.md):
   276	"Headless 层 (block core) — props schema (Zod) + MDX serialize/parse + 业务逻辑（无 React 依赖）"
   277	lives in `core/`, not in the foundation.
   278	
   279	```
   280	@skb/block-foundation             owns: BlockCoreDefinition shape (incl. mdxComponent: string)
   281	                                  owns: BlockRegistry routing table
   282	                                  does NOT own: serialize / parse functions
   283	
   284	@skb/block-callout/core           owns: calloutCore (registers via mdxComponent='Callout')
   285	                                  owns: serializeCallout(node) → mdast (Wave 2 add)
   286	                                  owns: parseCallout(mdast) → TiptapNode (Wave 2 add)
   287	
   288	@skb/mdx-bridge                   does the routing:
   289	                                  - parse: mdast.mdxJsxFlowElement{name:'Callout'} → call parseCallout
   290	                                  - serialize: TiptapNode{type:'callout'} → call serializeCallout
   291	```
   292	
   293	**Naming convention for Wave 2 block-* packages**:
   294	
   295	```typescript
   296	// packages/block-callout/src/core/index.ts
   297	export { calloutCore } from './core-definition';
   298	export { serializeCallout } from './serialize'; // (TiptapNode input → mdast output)
   299	export { parseCallout } from './parse';         // (mdast input → TiptapNode output)
   300	```
   301	
   302	mdx-bridge will add a routing table (Wave 2 mdx-bridge change, not in this
   303	RFC) keyed by `mdxComponent` name → resolved by `coreName` → calls the
   304	block's exported `serialize<BlockName>` / `parse<BlockName>`. Until then,
   305	mdx-bridge throws on unknown `mdxJsxFlowElement` per its existing
   306	[Fail-loud rule](../mdx-bridge/CONTRACT.md#fail-loud-rule)
   307	(`mdx-bridge: unsupported {block,inline,mark} type "<type>". Add a
   308	fixture and a parse + serialize case before introducing this <kind>.`).
   309	
   310	This RFC fixes the **`block-foundation` registration shape**; the
   311	serialize/parse routing on the `mdx-bridge` side is a separate Wave 2
   312	mdx-bridge PR (not this Track A2 PR). Block authors implementing C1+
   313	should expose `serialize<Block>` / `parse<Block>` from `core/index.ts`
   314	following the convention above so mdx-bridge can wire them when the
   315	routing table lands.
   316

JSON shape\n   106\t\n   107\tSpec §3.7 jsonc 例子展平 `type` / `command` 直接放在 matcher 同一层。Claude Code 实际 schema 要求每个 matcher entry 内部嵌套 `hooks: [{type, command}]` 数组。错误的 shape 会让 Claude Code 拒绝加载 Tier 0 orchestrator 是唯一与用户交互的接口；Codex worker 必须在 sandbox 内自动跑完，把输出交给 orchestrator review。\n   114\t\n   115\t**修正实现**：4 个 profile 全部 `approval_policy = \"never\"`；commit `7c77863`。\n   116\t\n 需要措辞松绑。\n   120\t\n   121\t**修正实现**：T0.3 fix round 选了保守路径——`~5.6.0`，lockfile pin 5.6.3；commit `b8ebbb6`。后续如想升级到 5.7/5.8/5.9，需另起 ADR 评估 tsc 行为变化。\n   122\t\n   123\t### Erratum 4: §3.10 lychee `include_verbatim`\n   124\t\n   125\tSpec §3.5 / plan T0.12 lychee 配置写 `include_verbatim = true`。但 spec / plan 文档本身含大量 fenced code 样例，里面的相对路径是 T0.13 等后续任务的\"未来文件\"占位符。`include_verbatim = true` 会让 lychee 把它们当真链接扫描，CI 必挂。\n   126\t\n   127\t**修正实现**：`include_verbatim = false`；commit `e92498f`。Trade-off：真实 code block 内的可执行链接也不会被检查；当前 corpus 没有此类用法，但若 Phase 1 引入\"教程式可执行代码块含真实外链\"，需要重新评估。\n   128\t\n   129\t### Erratum 5: T0.3 `tsconfig.json` 偏离 plan 字面量\n   130\t\n   131\tPlan T0.3 字面量 `{\"files\": [], \"references\": []}` 让 `tsc -b` 失败 `TS18002 'files' list in config file is empty`。\n   132\t\n   133\t**修正实现**：T0.3 实施时改为 `extends ./tsconfig.base.json` + `composite: false` + `noEmit: true` + `references: []`（commit `b8ebbb6`）。后续 T0.5 fix round 在添加 scripts 类型化覆盖时把 references 数组填为 `[{ 100\t\n   101\t## Spec errata (Phase 0 execution discoveries)\n   102\t\n   103\t执行 Phase 0 期间发现 5 处 spec 描述与实现需要校正。每条都已在对应 commit 落实，本 ADR 集中记录原因与替代实现：\n   104\t\n   105\t### Erratum 1: §3.7 hooks JSON shape\n   106\t\n   107\tSpec §3.7 jsonc 例子展平 `type` / `command` 直接放在 matcher 同一层。Claude Code 实际 schema 要求每个 matcher entry 内部嵌套 `hooks: [{type, command}]` 数组。错误的 shape 会让 Claude Code 拒绝加载 settings.json，报 `PostToolUse -> 0 -> hooks: Expected array, but received undefined`。\n   108\t\n   109\t**修正实现**：`scripts/render/settings-json.ts` 输出 nested-hooks shape；commit `860bb72`。\n   110\t\n   111\t### Erratum 2: §3.12 codex profile `approval_policy`\n   112\t\n   113\tSpec §3.12 jsonc 例子里 `[profiles.scaffolder]` 用 `approval_policy = \"on-request\"`，会让 Codex worker 中途暂停问用户。这违反单一卡点原则——Claude Tier 0 orchestrator 是唯一与用户交互的接口；Codex worker 必须在 sandbox 内自动跑完，把输出交给 orchestrator review。\n   114\t\n   115\t**修正实现**：4 个 profile 全部 `approval_policy = \"never\"`；commit `7c77863`。\n   116\t\n   117\t### Erratum 3: §1.2 TypeScript 版本\n   118\t\n   119\tSpec §1.2 描述 \"TypeScript 5.6 strict\"。Plan T0.3 用 `^5.6.0`，pnpm 解析到 5.9.3。如 5.6 是契约性版本（spec 字面意），应改 `~5.6.0`；如 spec 想表达 \"5.x strict\"，则 spec 需要措辞松绑。\n   120\t\n   121\t**修正实现**：T0.3 fix round 选了保守路径——`~5.6.0`，lockfile pin 5.6.3；commit `b8ebbb6`。后续如想升级到 5.7/5.8/5.9，需另起 ADR 评估 tsc 行为变化。\n   122\t\n   123\t### Erratum 4: §3.10 lychee `include_verbatim`\n   124\t\n   125\tSpec §3.5 / plan T0.12 lychee 配置写 `include_verbatim = true`。但 spec / plan 文档本身含大量 fenced code 样例，里面的相对路径是 T0.13 等后续任务的\"未来文件\"占位符。`include_verbatim = true` 会让 lychee 把它们当真链接扫描，CI 必挂。\n   126\t\n   127\t**修正实现**：`include_verbatim = false`；commit `e92498f`。Trade-off：真实 code block 内的可执行链接也不会被检查；当前 corpus 没有此类用法，但若 Phase 1 引入\"教程式可执行代码块含真实外链\"，需要重新评估。\n 132\t\n   133\t**修正实现**：T0.3 实施时改为 `extends ./tsconfig.base.json` + `composite: false` + `noEmit: true` + `references: []`（commit `b8ebbb6`）。后续 T0.5 fix round 在添加 scripts 类型化覆盖时把 references 数组填为 `[{ /bin/bash -lc 'nl -ba scripts/check-size-limits.mjs' in /home/weiyi/selfKnowledgeBaseWeb
 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	#!/usr/bin/env node
     2	/**
     3	 * 文件大小硬上限检查
     4	 * - 300 行 soft warn (ESLint 已处理 .ts/.tsx)
     5	 * - 500 行 hard fail (本脚本，覆盖所有源文件)
     6	 *
     7	 * Spec: docs/superpowers/specs/2026-04-29-self-knowledge-base-design.md §3.6
     8	 */
     9	
    10	import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
    11	import { execSync } from 'node:child_process';
    12	
    13	const HARD_LIMIT = 500;
    14	const EXTENSIONS = /\.(ts|tsx|js|mjs|jsx|py|astro|svelte)$/;
    15	
    16	const trackedFiles = execSync('git ls-files --cached --others --exclude-standard', {
    17	  encoding: 'utf8',
    18	})
    19	  .split('\n')
    20	  .filter((f) => f && EXTENSIONS.test(f));
    21	
    22	let violations = 0;
    23	for (const file of trackedFiles) {
    24	  const content = readFileSync(file, 'utf8');
    25	  const lines = content.endsWith('\n')
    26	    ? content.slice(0, -1).split('\n').length
    27	    : content.split('\n').length;
    28	  if (lines > HARD_LIMIT) {
    29	    console.error(`✗ ${file}: ${lines} lines (limit ${HARD_LIMIT})`);
    30	    violations++;
    31	  }
    32	}
    33	
    34	if (violations > 0) {
    35	  console.error(`\n${violations} file(s) exceed the ${HARD_LIMIT}-line hard limit.`);
    36	  process.exit(1);
    37	}
    38	console.log(`✓ All ${trackedFiles.length} source files under ${HARD_LIMIT} lines.`);

     1	import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
     2	import { renderMath } from '../ui-default/render-math';
     3	
     4	/**
     5	 * Byte-equality corpus for renderMath authority (ADR-0006 item #5).
     6	 *
     7	 * `renderMath` is the single import point for KaTeX in this package; both
     8	 * Math.tsx (React/NodeView path) and Math.astro (SSR path) call it. This
     9	 * suite exercises a representative input corpus to:
    10	 *   1. catch silent regressions if a future edit re-inlines `katex.renderToString`
    11	 *      with diverging options (drift detector for the algorithm-replication audit);
    12	 *   2. assert error-explicit behavior — invalid LaTeX must surface as
    13	 *      `.katex-error` in the output (spec D1 "red error box with the source
    14	 *      visible"; not silent fallback).
    15	 *
    16	 * KaTeX is the runtime authority — these tests do NOT pin the exact HTML byte
    17	 * shape (KaTeX patch versions can shift markup); they assert structural
    18	 * invariants (root class, displayMode discriminator, error-class presence)
    19	 * that any in-package option-divergence would break.
    20	 */
    21	describe('renderMath (KaTeX SSR authority)', () => {
    22	  it('renders inline expression with displayMode=false (no display block)', () => {
    23	    const html = renderMath('a^2 + b^2', false);
    24	    expect(html).toMatch(/class="katex"/);
    25	    expect(html).not.toMatch(/class="katex-display"/);
    26	  });
    27	
    28	  it('renders display expression with displayMode=true (wraps in katex-display)', () => {
    29	    const html = renderMath('\\sum_{i=0}^n x_i', true);
    30	    expect(html).toMatch(/class="katex-display"/);
    31	    expect(html).toMatch(/class="katex"/);
    32	  });
    33	
    34	  it('renders multiline displayed expression (multi-row aligned env)', () => {
    35	    const html = renderMath(
    36	      '\\begin{aligned} a &= 1 \\\\ b &= 2 \\end{aligned}',
    37	      true,
    38	    );
    39	    expect(html).toMatch(/class="katex-display"/);
    40	    // multi-line rendering produces multiple mord/mrel spans
    41	    expect(html).toMatch(/class="katex"/);
    42	    expect(html.length).toBeGreaterThan(100);
    43	  });
    44	
    45	  it('renders malformed LaTeX with errorColor inline + source visible (throwOnError=false invariant)', () => {
    46	    const html = renderMath('\\notARealCommand{x}', false);
    47	    // KaTeX with throwOnError=false renders unknown commands inline with
    48	    // errorColor (default #cc0000); source string is preserved verbatim so the
    49	    // user can see the offending expression (spec D1 "red error box with the
    50	    // source visible"). math.css uses [data-block='math'] [style*="cc0000"]
    51	    // upgrade selector to bind --color-error token + bordered box.
    52	    expect(html).toMatch(/class="katex"/);
    53	    expect(html).toMatch(/notARealCommand/);
    54	    expect(html).toMatch(/#cc0000/);
    55	  });
    56	
    57	  it('inline + display same expression produce different output (displayMode discriminator)', () => {
    58	    const expr = '\\frac{1}{2}';
    59	    const inlineHtml = renderMath(expr, false);
    60	    const displayHtml = renderMath(expr, true);
    61	    expect(inlineHtml).not.toBe(displayHtml);
    62	    expect(displayHtml).toMatch(/class="katex-display"/);
    63	    expect(inlineHtml).not.toMatch(/class="katex-display"/);
    64	  });
    65	});
